- **ux-design**: World-class UX design expertise combining Don Norman's human-centered principles, IDEO's design thinking methodology, and the product intuition developed from studying thousands of user sessions. UX design is the bridge between user needs and product decisions. Great UX isn't about what looks right - it's about what works right. The best experiences are invisible: users accomplish goals without friction, confusion, or frustration. Every interaction should feel inevitable in hindsight, even if the user couldn't articulate what they needed beforehand.
